## STANDING CORRECTION (parent, 2026-10-26 — applies to ALL W45 cores)

- Core ABSTRACT language: a non-Chinese target's CORE edition is its zh-cn edition (explanation layer is Chinese), so its ABSTRACT.txt must be in Simplified Chinese — verified against W43 repo index (2026-w43-it-zh-cn / en-zh-cn / de-zh-cn / fr-zh-cn abstracts are all Chinese). The W45 core builder briefs wrongly said "target language" — IT's proofreader caught it. IT fix round rewrites its abstract to Chinese. EN/DE/ES/NL/PT abstracts (currently target-language) MUST be rewritten to Simplified Chinese before any repo insert — fold into each core's next fix round, or run a standalone abstract correction if a core otherwise finalizes first. Batch C cores (RU/JA/KO): Chinese abstracts. Batch D cores (zh-cn/zh-tw, explanation layer English): English abstracts.
- Readers'-corner disclosure standard (established across EN/ES/NL/PT): deferral of the W43 "real letters from VOL.2" promise must be disclosed ON THE PAGE in target + reader-native languages; KIT-only disclosure does not pass; fabrication never passes.
